# Generation prompts

## art/batch/scenes/class/barbarian-pc.png
+
Barbarians of several species at a wind-swept tundra camp under a stormy sky: one splits logs with a great axe, two wrestle in bloody snow, one beats a big hide drum by a bonfire, war banners on spears.

Vivid sunset with purple and pink clouds illuminates the snowy tundra; retain brilliant violet-pink snow and turquoise floodwater rather than orange monotone. A flood has swallowed half the adjoining settlement; roofs and floating broken timbers remain at the far left. Over the shoulder of a large orc barbarian at the left edge, see the camp: log splitter on the far left, two bloodied adult wrestlers and a drum player by a small bonfire on the right. Several adults have conspicuous bleeding beast-bite wounds; they grieve lost homes. Two strangers share bread beside the drum. No beast or other enemy visible.

Studio Ghibli 2D anime film look, bright colorful environment, bloody gore and despair; thin hand-drawn outlines, two or three cel tones on expressive characters, lush gouache backgrounds.

2400x1344 landscape app background. Over-shoulder camera from far left; low horizon with open purple-pink sky across center33–67%, quiet broad unmarked snow below it. All people and busy details on the outer thirds, left side heavier than right. Four outer edges fade into loose painted brush strokes and dark paper. No text, writing, runes, logos, UI, copied film characters, photorealism or 3D.

## art/batch/scenes/class/bard-pc.png
+
Bards in a lantern-lit open-air festival square at dusk: a halfling plays a lute on a small wooden stage, an elf dances with ribbons, a tiefling juggles, children and townsfolk clap, bunting overhead.

Bright clear blue morning sky above the festival square, colorful bunting and lanterns unlit in daylight. A close medium group occupies the left side: a sorrowful halfling plays lute on a low wooden stage while an adult friend carries a bleeding wounded adult comrade past it. At the far right an elf dances with ribbons and a tiefling juggles to reassure frightened but unharmed children and exhausted clapping townsfolk. A clear shallow stream at the left edge runs visibly red with blood. Enormous terrifying beholders hover above the ruined keep in the upper-left distance, dominating the small desperate inhabitants. ## art/batch/scenes/class/cleric-pc.png
+
Clerics at a hilltop chapel at sunrise: one heals a farmer's injured arm with soft golden light, one rings a bronze bell, one lights candles at a stone altar, a rose window glowing.

Bright desert daylight, saturated blue sky and red sandstone around the hilltop chapel, overriding sunrise. View the clerics as dark cel-shaded silhouettes rimmed against the brilliant sky, with enough face detail to read grief. Left side: a healer holds a dying adult farmer's hand while soft golden healing light touches the injured arm; vivid blood soaks the white desert flowers around them. Right edge: cleric rings the bronze bell and another lights altar candles beneath the glowing rose window. A vast terrifying dragon passes overhead, its broad shadow crossing the land. Frame a broad sunlit red sandstone chapel wall as the calm center; no central sunset or river.

Studio Ghibli 2D anime film look, bright colorful environment, bloody gore and despair; thin hand-drawn outlines, two or three cel tones on expressive characters, lush gouache backgrounds.

2400x1344 landscape app background. Low silhouette camera looking upward; high sky and asymmetric architecture, no central vanishing point. Dragon and other busy forms kept to side thirds; all four outer edges fade into loose brush strokes and dark paper. No text, writing, runes, logos, UI, copied film characters, photorealism or 3D.


# batch-001 v3

## art/batch/scenes/class/barbarian-pc.png

Bright turquoise and white light gleams across the camp's windswept snow and ice. Look over the shoulder of the huge half-orc at the left foreground, his split shield framing the scene. A cursed beast lies dead and visibly rotting beside the far-right camp perimeter, not attacking. A wounded adult warrior presses a red-soaked cloth firmly against his side. Small at the right edge, a soldier tilts his last waterskin to a wounded adult stranger's lips. Their exhausted, heartbroken expressions carry the loss.

Barbarians of several species at a wind-swept tundra camp: a huge half-orc with a split shield guarding the camp, a woman binding a gash on her thigh with fur strips, an old warrior beating a hide drum for the fallen, broken war banners on spears.

loose energetic hand-drawn sketch illustration (ink lines, flat textured color, hard shadows, expressive cartoon faces), bright and colorful environment, bloody gore and despair

2400x1344 landscape desktop background. Middle third of width low-detail and free of faces for app cards. Characters and busy details go to the sides asymmetrically. Bright and colorful. Use a broad unmarked turquoise-white snowbank and pale ice across the middle third, not a central road. Cluster the half-orc and camp activity strongly on the left, with the drummer, wounded people and beast at the right edge; keep the large foreground shoulder at the far left. A high horizon leaves the quiet snow dominant. All four outer edges fade softly into loose brush strokes on light cream paper. Keep figures and key details inside the middle80% of height so the final crop can remove some top and bottom.

Loose, energetic hand-drawn digital sketch illustration with confident black ink lines with some visible rough construction strokes, slightly exaggerated cartoon proportions and very expressive faces, flat color fills with dry-brush and pencil texture, hard-edged cel shadows with thin red-brown accent strokes in the shading, like one finished frame of modern Western animation concept art (a single illustration, no turnaround, no model sheet, no labels). Not anime, not Studio Ghibli, not painterly realism.

Every person is an adult. Bloody gore and despair in a bright vivid colorful world, never a dark, gloomy, grey or orange-only environment. Flat textured environment fills match the characters. Vivid red blood; bodily wounds and emotional loss, grief and a world being destroyed. The warmth moment stays small and to one side. An enemy appears only when the Subject or Variation names one, at its specified size and distance. Ordinary animals remain natural and alive or wounded, never monstrous.

WOW at first glance: striking, full of character and vivid color, clear silhouettes, confident gesture and composition. Specific story details, varied adult ages, bodies and clothes, natural anatomy and hands. Avoid random ribbons, flowers or bows added for no reason; a generic glowing star or orb as the whole idea; mushy or blurry details; broken anatomy; extra fingers; warped perspective; copy-paste duplicates; meaningless ornament; plastic skin; text-like squiggles; bland stock composition.
